**Working**

The marks on the number line are $\dfrac {3} {10}$, $\dfrac {4} {10}$, $\dfrac {5} {10}$ (simplified to $\dfrac {1} {2}$) and $\dfrac {6} {10}$ (simplified to $\dfrac {3} {5}$).

$
\begin{aligned}
\text{Arrow mark} &= \dfrac {4} {10} \\\\
&= \dfrac {2 \times 2} {2 \times 5} \\\\
&= \dfrac {\cancel{2} \times 2} {\cancel{2} \times 5} \\\\
&= \dfrac {2} {5}
\end{aligned}
$
</div>
</div>
<div class="answers">
<div class="answer" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Answer**

$\dfrac {2} {5}$

</div>
</div>

<li class="questionContainer">
<div class="question_envelope rag_not_found subsubquestion">
<div class="question subsubquestion" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Question**

Chan now empties the jug of water by pouring equal amounts into six identical empty beakers. 
One of the beakers is shown in the diagram.

Draw a line on the beaker to indicate the level of water in it.

</div>
<div class="questioncodeblock" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">
<div class="questioncodeblock_title">
<strong style="display: block; margin-bottom: 0.5rem;">Question Raw</strong>
</div>
<pre class="language-latex"><code class="language-latex">Chan now empties the jug of water by pouring equal amounts into six identical empty beakers. 
One of the beakers is shown in the diagram.
<emptyline>
Draw a line on the beaker to indicate the level of water in it.
</code></pre>
</div>
<div class="workings">
<div class="tips placeholder" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Tips**

<div class="tip">
%empty%
</div>
</div>
<div class="formulas placeholder" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Formulas**

<div class="formula">
%empty%
</div>
</div>
<div class="working" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Working**

$
\begin{aligned}
\text {Water in each beaker}                      &= \dfrac {\text{total water}} {\text{total beakers}} \\\\
                                                  &= \dfrac {1800} {6} \\\\
                                                  &= 300 \text {ml} \\\\
\text {Water in one division of beaker}           &= \dfrac {\text{total capacity}} {\text{number of divisions}} \\\\
                                                  &= \dfrac {400} {8} \\\\
                                                  &= \dfrac {8 \times 50} {8} \\\\
                                                  &= \dfrac {\cancel{8} \times 50} {\cancel{8}} \\\\
                                                  &= 50 \\\\
\text{Number of divisions containing 300ml water} &= \dfrac {300} {50} \\\\
                                                  &= \dfrac {50 \times 6} {50} \\\\
                                                  &= \dfrac {\cancel{50} \times 6} {50} \\\\
                                                  &= 6
\end{aligned}
$

6th division is to be marked.
</div>
</div>
<div class="answers">
<div class="answer" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Answer**

$6$

</div>
</div>
<div class="workingscodeblock">
<div class="tipcodeblock" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">
<div class="tipcodeblock_title">
<strong style="display: block; margin-bottom: 0.5rem;">Tips Raw</strong>
</div>
<pre class="language-latex"><code class="language-latex">%empty%
</code></pre>
</div>

<div class="workingcodeblock_title">
<strong style="display: block; margin-bottom: 0.5rem;">Working Raw</strong>
</div>
<pre class="language-latex"><code class="language-latex">$
\begin{aligned}
\text {Water in each beaker}                      &= \dfrac {\text{total water}} {\text{total beakers}} \\\\
                                                  &= \dfrac {1800} {6} \\\\
                                                  &= 300 \text {ml} \\\\
\text {Water in one division of beaker}           &= \dfrac {\text{total capacity}} {\text{number of divisions}} \\\\
                                                  &= \dfrac {400} {8} \\\\
                                                  &= \dfrac {8 \times 50} {8} \\\\
                                                  &= \dfrac {\cancel{8} \times 50} {\cancel{8}} \\\\
                                                  &= 50 \\\\
\text{Number of divisions containing 300ml water} &= \dfrac {300} {50} \\\\
                                                  &= \dfrac {50 \times 6} {50} \\\\
                                                  &= \dfrac {\cancel{50} \times 6} {50} \\\\
                                                  &= 6
\end{aligned}
$
<emptyline>
6th division is to be marked.
</code></pre>
</div>
</div>
<div class="answers">
<div class="answercodeblock" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">
<div class="answercodeblock_title">
<strong style="display: block; margin-bottom: 0.5rem;">Option Raw</strong>
</div>

<div class="working" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Working**

$
\begin{aligned}
\text {Total small triangles}       &= 4 \times 4 \\\\
                                    &= 16 \\\\
\text {Shaded}                      &= 8 \\\\
\text{Fraction shaded}              &= \dfrac {8} {16} \\\\
                                    &= \dfrac {8} {2 \times 8} \\\\
                                    &= \dfrac {\cancel{8}} {2 \times \cancel{8}} \\\\
                                    &= \dfrac {1} {2}
\end{aligned}
$
</div>
</div>
<div class="answers">
<div class="answer" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Answer**

$\dfrac {1} {2}$

</div>

**Question**

$A + B$?

</div>
<div class="questioncodeblock" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">
<div class="questioncodeblock_title">
<strong style="display: block; margin-bottom: 0.5rem;">Question Raw</strong>
</div>
<pre class="language-latex"><code class="language-latex">$A + B$?
</code></pre>
</div>
<div class="workings">
<div class="tips placeholder" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Tips**

<div class="tip">
%empty%
</div>
</div>
<div class="formulas placeholder" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Formulas**

<div class="formula">
%empty%
</div>
</div>
<div class="working" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Working**

As per the diagram,

$
\begin{aligned}
F     &= \dfrac {1} {8} \\\\
B     &= \dfrac {1} {2} - \dfrac {1} {8} \\\\
      &= \dfrac {4} {2 \times 4} - \dfrac {1} {8} \\\\
      &= \dfrac {4} {8} - \dfrac {1} {8} \\\\
      &= \dfrac {4 - 1} {8} \\\\
      &= \dfrac {3} {8} \\\\
A + B &= \dfrac {1} {4} + \dfrac {3} {8} \\\\
      &= \dfrac {2} {4 \times 2} + \dfrac {3} {8} \\\\
      &= \dfrac {2} {8} + \dfrac {3} {8} \\\\
      &= \dfrac {2 + 3} {8} \\\\
      &= \dfrac {5} {8} 
\end{aligned}
$
</div>
</div>
<div class="answers">
<div class="answer" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Answer**

$\dfrac {5} {8}$

</div>
</div>

      &= \dfrac {4} {2 \times 4} - \dfrac {1} {8} \\\\

</div>
<div class="questioncodeblock" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">
<div class="questioncodeblock_title">
<strong style="display: block; margin-bottom: 0.5rem;">Question Raw</strong>

</code></pre>

<div class="tips placeholder" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

<div class="working" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Working**

As per the diagram,

$
\begin{aligned}
F+E+D       &= \dfrac {1}{4} \\\\
B           &= \dfrac {3}{8} \\\\
B+F+E+D     &= \dfrac {1}{4} + \dfrac {3}{8} \\\\
            &= \dfrac {1 \times 2}{4 \times 2} + \dfrac {3}{8} \\\\
            &= \dfrac {2}{8} + \dfrac {3}{8} \\\\
            &= \dfrac {2+3}{8} \\\\
            &= \dfrac {5} {8}
\end{aligned}
$
</div>
</div>

<div class="answer" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

<li class="questionContainer">
<div class="question_envelope rag_not_found subquestion">
<div class="question subquestion" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Question**

![papers/city-of-london-school/2020/sample-paper-1/s01-q08-s01-i00.png](/assets/papers/city-of-london-school/2020/sample-paper-1/s01-q08-s01-i00.png "papers/city-of-london-school/2020/sample-paper-1/s01-q08-s01-i00.png")

What fraction of the above design is shaded? Give your fraction in its simplest form.

</div>
<div class="questioncodeblock" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">
<div class="questioncodeblock_title">
<strong style="display: block; margin-bottom: 0.5rem;">Question Raw</strong>
</div>
<pre class="language-latex"><code class="language-latex">%image%
<emptyline>
What fraction of the above design is shaded? Give your fraction in its simplest form.
</code></pre>
</div>
<div class="workings">
<div class="tips placeholder" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

</div>
</div>
<div class="formulas placeholder" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

<div class="formula">
%empty%
</div>
</div>
<div class="working" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Working**

$
\begin{aligned}
\text{Fraction shaded}  &= \dfrac {4} {12} \\\\
                        &= \dfrac {4} {4 \times 3} \\\\
                        &= \dfrac {\cancel{4}} {\cancel{4} \times 3} \\\\
                        &= \dfrac {1} {3}
\end{aligned}
$
</div>
</div>
<div class="answers">
<div class="answer" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Answer**

$\dfrac {1} {3}$

</div>
</div>

<li class="questionContainer">
<div class="question_envelope rag_not_found subquestion">
<div class="question subquestion" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Question**

How many more of the hexagons need to be shaded so that $\dfrac{3} {4}$ of the design is shaded? 

</div>
<div class="questioncodeblock" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">
<div class="questioncodeblock_title">
<strong style="display: block; margin-bottom: 0.5rem;">Question Raw</strong>
</div>
<pre class="language-latex"><code class="language-latex">How many more of the hexagons need to be shaded so that $\dfrac{3} {4}$ of the design is shaded? 
</code></pre>
</div>
<div class="workings">
<div class="tips placeholder" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Tips**

<div class="tip">
%empty%
</div>
</div>
<div class="formulas placeholder" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Formulas**

<div class="formula">
%empty%
</div>
</div>
<div class="working" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Working**

$
\begin{aligned}
\text{Fraction shaded}  &= \dfrac {3} {4} \times 12  - 4 \\\\
                        &= \dfrac {3 \times 12} {4} - 4 \\\\
                        &= \dfrac {3 \times 3 \times 4} {4} - 4 \\\\
                        &= \dfrac {3 \times 3 \times \cancel{4}} {\cancel{4}} - 4 \\\\
                        &= 3 \times 3- 4 \\\\
                        &= 9 - 4 \\\\
                        &= 5
\end{aligned}
$
</div>
</div>
<div class="answers">
<div class="answer" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Answer**

$5$

</div>
</div>
<div class="workingscodeblock">
<div class="tipcodeblock" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">
<div class="tipcodeblock_title">
<strong style="display: block; margin-bottom: 0.5rem;">Tips Raw</strong>
</div>
<pre class="language-latex"><code class="language-latex">%empty%
</code></pre>
</div>
<div class="formulacodeblock" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">
<div class="formulacodeblock_title">
<strong style="display: block; margin-bottom: 0.5rem;">Formulas Raw</strong>
</div>
<pre class="language-latex"><code class="language-latex">%empty%
</code></pre>
</div>
<div class="workingcodeblock" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">
<div class="workingcodeblock_title">
<strong style="display: block; margin-bottom: 0.5rem;">Working Raw</strong>
</div>
<pre class="language-latex"><code class="language-latex">$
\begin{aligned}
\text{Fraction shaded}  &= \dfrac {3} {4} \times 12  - 4 \\\\
                        &= \dfrac {3 \times 12} {4} - 4 \\\\
                        &= \dfrac {3 \times 3 \times 4} {4} - 4 \\\\
                        &= \dfrac {3 \times 3 \times \cancel{4}} {\cancel{4}} - 4 \\\\
                        &= 3 \times 3- 4 \\\\
                        &= 9 - 4 \\\\
                        &= 5
\end{aligned}
$
</code></pre>
</div>
</div>

<div class="question" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Question**

The diagram shows a design formed by drawing six lines in a regular hexagon. The lines divide each edge of the hexagon into three equal parts. What fraction of the hexagon is shaded?

![papers/dulwich-college/sample/specimen-paper-g/s01-q18-i00.png](/assets/papers/dulwich-college/sample/specimen-paper-g/s01-q18-i00.png "papers/dulwich-college/sample/specimen-paper-g/s01-q18-i00.png")

</div>
<div class="questioncodeblock" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">
<div class="questioncodeblock_title">
<strong style="display: block; margin-bottom: 0.5rem;">Question Raw</strong>
</div>
<pre class="language-latex"><code class="language-latex">The diagram shows a design formed by drawing six lines in a regular hexagon. The lines divide each edge of the hexagon into three equal parts. What fraction of the hexagon is shaded?
<emptyline>
%image%
</code></pre>
</div>
<div class="workings">
<div class="tips placeholder" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Tips**

<div class="tip">
%empty%
</div>
</div>
<div class="formulas placeholder" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Formulas**

<div class="formula">
%empty%
</div>
</div>
<div class="working" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Working**

$
\begin{aligned}
\text {number of triangles in one hexagon} &= 6 \\\\
\text {total triangles}                    &= 6 \times 7 + 12 \\\\
                                           &= 42 + 12 \\\\
                                           &= 54 \\\\
\text {fraction of hexagon shaded}         &= \dfrac {12} {54} \\\\
                                           &= \dfrac {2 \times 6} {6 \times 9} \\\\
                                           &= \dfrac {2 \times \cancel{6}} {\cancel{6} \times 9} \\\\
                                           &= \dfrac {2} {9}
\end{aligned}
$
</div>
</div>
<div class="answers">
<div class="answer" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Answer**

$\dfrac {2} {9}$

</div>
</div>

$

<div class="question subquestion" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Question**

$\dfrac{2}{3} + \dfrac{5}{6}$

</div>
<div class="questioncodeblock" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">
<div class="questioncodeblock_title">
<strong style="display: block; margin-bottom: 0.5rem;">Question Raw</strong>
</div>
<pre class="language-latex"><code class="language-latex">$\dfrac{2}{3} + \dfrac{5}{6}$
</code></pre>
</div>
<div class="workings">
<div class="tips placeholder" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Tips**

<div class="tip">
%empty%
</div>
</div>
<div class="formulas placeholder" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

<div class="formula">
%empty%
</div>
</div>
<div class="working" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Working**

$
\begin{aligned}
&= \dfrac {2} {3} + \dfrac {5} {6} \\\\
&= \dfrac {2 \times 2} {3 \times 2} + \dfrac {5} {6} \\\\
&= \dfrac {4} {6} + \dfrac {5} {6} \\\\
&= \dfrac {4 + 5} {6} \\\\
&= \dfrac {9} {6} \\\\
&= \dfrac {3 \times 3} {3 \times 2} \\\\
&= \dfrac {\cancel {3} \times 3} {\cancel {3} \times 2} \\\\
&= \dfrac {3} {2} \\\\
&= 1 \dfrac {1} {2}
\end{aligned}
$
</div>
</div>
<div class="answers">
<div class="answer" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Answer**

$1 \dfrac {1} {2}$

</div>

&= \dfrac {10 - 9} {12} \\\\
&= \dfrac {1} {12}
\end{aligned}
$

<div class="question subquestion" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Question**

In an orchestra of $24$ children, $\dfrac{3} {4}$ of them are girls. How many are boys?

</div>
<div class="questioncodeblock" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">
<div class="questioncodeblock_title">
<strong style="display: block; margin-bottom: 0.5rem;">Question Raw</strong>
</div>
<pre class="language-latex"><code class="language-latex">In an orchestra of $24$ children, $\dfrac{3} {4}$ of them are girls. How many are boys?
</code></pre>
</div>
<div class="workings">
<div class="tips placeholder" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Tips**

<div class="tip">
%empty%
</div>
</div>
<div class="formulas placeholder" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Formulas**

<div class="formula">
%empty%
</div>
</div>
<div class="working" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Working**

$
\begin{aligned}
\text {Fraction of boys} &= 1 - \dfrac {3} {4} \\\\
                         &= \dfrac {1} {4} \\\\
\text {Number of boys}   &= \dfrac {1} {4} \times 24 \\\\
                         &= \dfrac {24} {4} \\\\
                         &= \dfrac {6 \times 4} {4} \\\\
                         &= \dfrac {6 \times \cancel{4}} {\cancel{4}} \\\\
                         &= 6
\end{aligned}
$
</div>
</div>
<div class="answers">
<div class="answer" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Answer**

$6$

</div>
</div>
<div class="workingscodeblock">
<div class="tipcodeblock" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">
<div class="tipcodeblock_title">
<strong style="display: block; margin-bottom: 0.5rem;">Tips Raw</strong>
</div>
<pre class="language-latex"><code class="language-latex">%empty%
</code></pre>
</div>
<div class="formulacodeblock" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">
<div class="formulacodeblock_title">
<strong style="display: block; margin-bottom: 0.5rem;">Formulas Raw</strong>
</div>
<pre class="language-latex"><code class="language-latex">%empty%
</code></pre>
</div>
<div class="workingcodeblock" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">
<div class="workingcodeblock_title">
<strong style="display: block; margin-bottom: 0.5rem;">Working Raw</strong>
</div>
<pre class="language-latex"><code class="language-latex">$
\begin{aligned}
\text {Fraction of boys} &= 1 - \dfrac {3} {4} \\\\
                         &= \dfrac {1} {4} \\\\
\text {Number of boys}   &= \dfrac {1} {4} \times 24 \\\\
                         &= \dfrac {24} {4} \\\\
                         &= \dfrac {6 \times 4} {4} \\\\
                         &= \dfrac {6 \times \cancel{4}} {\cancel{4}} \\\\
                         &= 6
\end{aligned}
$
</code></pre>
</div>
</div>
<div class="answers">
<div class="answercodeblock" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">
<div class="answercodeblock_title">

</div>

</div>
<div class="answercodeblock" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">
<div class="answercodeblock_title">
<strong style="display: block; margin-bottom: 0.5rem;">Key Raw</strong>
</div>
<pre class="language-latex"><code class="language-latex">%empty%</code></pre>
</div>
<div class="answercodeblock" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">
<div class="answercodeblock_title">
<strong style="display: block; margin-bottom: 0.5rem;">Answer Raw</strong>
</div>
<pre class="language-latex"><code class="language-latex">$6$
</code></pre>
</div>
</div>

</div>
</li>
<li class="questionContainer">

$50\%$ of the trees in a wood are fir trees. If there are $450$ trees in the wood, how many fir trees are there?

</div>
<div class="questioncodeblock" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

</div>
<div class="papername">
<p>sevenoaks-school--11-plus--maths--2015--sample-paper-6:1:14</p>
</div>
<div class="rag">
<p>Waffles Woof</p>

<div class="rag">

</div>
<div class="rag">

<li>

</li>
<li>
tareaperimeter

</div>
<div class="question" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Question**

In the diagram the small squares are all the same size. What fraction of the large square is shaded?

![papers/sevenoaks-school/2015/sample-paper-6/s01-q14-i00.png](/assets/papers/sevenoaks-school/2015/sample-paper-6/s01-q14-i00.png "papers/sevenoaks-school/2015/sample-paper-6/s01-q14-i00.png")

</div>
<div class="questioncodeblock" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">
<div class="questioncodeblock_title">
<strong style="display: block; margin-bottom: 0.5rem;">Question Raw</strong>
</div>
<pre class="language-latex"><code class="language-latex">In the diagram the small squares are all the same size. What fraction of the large square is shaded?
<emptyline>
%image%
</code></pre>
</div>
<div class="workings">
<div class="tips placeholder" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Tips**

<div class="tip">
%empty%
</div>
</div>
<div class="formulas placeholder" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Formulas**

<div class="formula">
%empty%
</div>
</div>
<div class="working" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Working**

As per the figure,

If we look at the large square, we can see that $8$ portions have been shaded and there are $18$ portions in total.

The fraction that has been shaded $= \dfrac {8} {18}$

Let's find out what fraction of the large square is shaded.

$
\begin{aligned}
&= \dfrac {8} {18} \\\\
&= \dfrac {4 \times 2} {9 \times 2} \\\\
&= \dfrac {4 \times \cancel{2}} {9 \times \cancel{2}} \\\\
&= \dfrac {4} {9}
\end{aligned}
$

Fraction of large square that has been shaded in simple fraction $= \dfrac {4} {9}$
</div>
</div>
<div class="answers">

<pre class="language-latex"><code class="language-latex">As per the figure,
<emptyline>
If we look at the large square, we can see that $8$ portions have been shaded and there are $18$ portions in total.
<emptyline>
The fraction that has been shaded $= \dfrac {8} {18}$
<emptyline>
Let's find out what fraction of the large square is shaded.
<emptyline>
$
\begin{aligned}
&= \dfrac {8} {18} \\\\
&= \dfrac {4 \times 2} {9 \times 2} \\\\
&= \dfrac {4 \times \cancel{2}} {9 \times \cancel{2}} \\\\
&= \dfrac {4} {9}
\end{aligned}
$
<emptyline>
Fraction of large square that has been shaded in simple fraction $= \dfrac {4} {9}$
</code></pre>

<div class="uuid">

<div class="papername">

<div class="rag">

<div class="topics">

</li>

<div class="question" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Question**

![papers/sydenham-high-school/sample/sample-paper-1/s01-q17-i00.png](/assets/papers/sydenham-high-school/sample/sample-paper-1/s01-q17-i00.png "papers/sydenham-high-school/sample/sample-paper-1/s01-q17-i00.png")

This diagram is made up of $7$ identical regular hexagons and $12$ identical equilateral triangles.

Note: the sides of the triangles are the same length as the sides of the hexagons.

Find the fraction of the diagram that is shaded. 

</div>
<div class="questioncodeblock" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">
<div class="questioncodeblock_title">
<strong style="display: block; margin-bottom: 0.5rem;">Question Raw</strong>
</div>
<pre class="language-latex"><code class="language-latex">%image%
<emptyline>
This diagram is made up of $7$ identical regular hexagons and $12$ identical equilateral triangles.
<emptyline>
Note: the sides of the triangles are the same length as the sides of the hexagons.
<emptyline>
Find the fraction of the diagram that is shaded. 
</code></pre>
</div>
<div class="workings">
<div class="tips placeholder" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Tips**

<div class="tip">
%empty%
</div>
</div>
<div class="formulas placeholder" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Formulas**

<div class="formula">
%empty%
</div>
</div>
<div class="working" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Working**

$
\begin{aligned}
&= \dfrac {\text {Shaded portion} } {\text {Total portion} } \\\\
&= \dfrac {7} {12 + 7} \\\\
&= \dfrac {7} {19}
\end{aligned}
$
</div>
</div>
<div class="answers">
<div class="answer" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Answer**

$\dfrac {7} {19}$

</div>
</div>

What fraction of the triangle is now not shaded?

Give your answer in its simplest form.

</div>
<div class="questioncodeblock" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">
<div class="questioncodeblock_title">

</div>

<emptyline>
Give your answer in its simplest form.
</code></pre>
</div>

%empty%
</div>
</div>
<div class="working" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Working**

$
\begin{aligned}
\text{Not shaded}   &= \dfrac {3} {6} \\\\
                    &= \dfrac {3} {3 \times 2} \\\\
                    &= \dfrac {\cancel{3} } {\cancel{3} \times 2} \\\\
                    &= \dfrac {1} {2}
\end{aligned}
$
</div>
</div>
<div class="answers">
<div class="answer" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Answer**

$\dfrac {1} {2}$

</div>

**Question**

Complete the table above.

</div>
<div class="questioncodeblock" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">
<div class="questioncodeblock_title">

<div class="workings">

</div>
</div>
<div class="working" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Working**

Given that,

$
\begin{aligned}
\text{Right-handed players in Team Alpha} \ &= 7
\end{aligned}
$

$
\begin{aligned}
\text{Left-handed players in Team Beta} \ &= 2
\end{aligned}
$

$
\begin{aligned}
\text{Total left-handed players} \ &= 6 \\\\
\text{Total left-handed players} &= \text{Left-handed players in Team Beta} + \text{Left-handed players in Team Alpha} \\\\
2 + \text{Left-handed players in Team Alpha} &= 6 \\\\
\text{Left-handed players in Team Alpha} &= 4
\end{aligned}
$

$
\begin{aligned}
\text{Total players in Team Alpha} \ &= \text{Left-handed players in Team Alpha} + \text{Right-handed players in Team Alpha} \\\\
\text{Total players in Team Alpha} \ &= 4 + 7 \\\\
\text{Total players in Team Alpha} \ &= 11
\end{aligned}
$

$
\begin{aligned}
\text{Left-handed players in Team Beta} + \text{Right-handed players in Team Beta} &= \text{Total players in Team Beta} \ \\\\
\text{Left-handed players in Team Beta} + \text{Right-handed players in Team Beta} &= 11 \\\\
2 + \text{Right-handed players in Team Beta} &= 11 \\\\
\text{Right-handed players in Team Beta} &= 9
\end{aligned}
$

$
\begin{aligned}
\text{Total players playing} &= \text{Total players in Team Alpha} + \text{Total players in Team Beta} \\\\
\text{Total players playing} &= 11 + 11 \\\\
\text{Total players playing} &= 22
\end{aligned}
$
</div>
</div>
<div class="answers">
<div class="answer" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Answer**

$
\begin{array}{lllllll}
                    & \text{RH} \ & \text{LH} \ & \text{total} \ \\
\text{Team Alpha} \ &  7          &  4          &   11           \\
\text{Team Beta} \  &  9          &  2          &   11           \\
\text{total} \      & 16          &  6          &   22           \\
\end{array}
$

</div>
<div class="answer" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Answer**

**Question**

Which is larger: $\dfrac{3}{5}$ or $\dfrac{2}{3}$

Give a reason for your answer.

</div>
<div class="questioncodeblock" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">
<div class="questioncodeblock_title">
<strong style="display: block; margin-bottom: 0.5rem;">Question Raw</strong>
</div>
<pre class="language-latex"><code class="language-latex">Which is larger: $\dfrac{3}{5}$ or $\dfrac{2}{3}$
<emptyline>
Give a reason for your answer.
</code></pre>
</div>
<div class="workings">
<div class="tips placeholder" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

<div class="tip">
%empty%
</div>
</div>
<div class="formulas placeholder" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Formulas**

<div class="working" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

</div>
</div>
<div class="working" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Working**

when we take LCM of both fraction we will get

$
\begin{aligned}
&=\dfrac{3}{5}     &&&&&&&   &=\dfrac{2}{3} \\\\
&=\dfrac{3\times 3}{5\times3}    &&&&&&&  &=\dfrac{2\times 5}{3 \times 5} \\\\\
&=\dfrac{9}{15}    &&&&&&&  &=\dfrac{10}{15} \
\end{aligned}
$

So,
$
\dfrac{2}{3} > \dfrac{3}{5}
$
</div>
</div>
<div class="answers">
<div class="answer" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Answer**

$\dfrac{2}{3}$

</div>
</div>

**Question**

Arrange these fractions in order from **smallest** to **largest**.

$1 \dfrac{1}{3} \quad \dfrac{2}{3} \quad \dfrac{4}{5} \quad \dfrac{13}{15} \quad \dfrac{3}{5}$

</div>
<div class="questioncodeblock" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">
<div class="questioncodeblock_title">
<strong style="display: block; margin-bottom: 0.5rem;">Question Raw</strong>
</div>
<pre class="language-latex"><code class="language-latex">Arrange these fractions in order from **smallest** to **largest**.
<emptyline>
$1 \dfrac{1}{3} \quad \dfrac{2}{3} \quad \dfrac{4}{5} \quad \dfrac{13}{15} \quad \dfrac{3}{5}$
</code></pre>

</div>

<div class="working" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Working**

Let's make the denominator the same for all the numbers, so we can compare them.

$
\begin{aligned}
1 \dfrac{1}{3} \quad \dfrac{2}{3} \quad \dfrac{4}{5} \quad \dfrac{13}{15} \quad \dfrac{3}{5} \\\\\\\
\dfrac{4}{3} \quad \dfrac{2}{3} \quad \dfrac{4}{5} \quad \dfrac{13}{15} \quad \dfrac{3}{5} \\\\\\\
\dfrac{4 \times 5}{3 \times 5} \quad \dfrac{2 \times 5}{3 \times 5} \quad \dfrac{4 \times 3}{5 \times 3} \quad \dfrac{13}{15} \quad \dfrac{3 \times 3}{5 \times 3} \\\\\\\
\dfrac{20}{15} \quad \dfrac{10}{15} \quad \dfrac{12}{15} \quad \dfrac{13}{15} \quad \dfrac{9}{15} 
\end{aligned}
$

Let's put them in ascending order now.

$
\begin{aligned}
\dfrac{9}{15} \quad \dfrac{10}{15} \quad \dfrac{12}{15} \quad \dfrac{13}{15} \quad \dfrac{20}{15} 
\end{aligned}
$
</div>
</div>
<div class="answers">
<div class="answer" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Answer**

</div>

<div class="question" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Question**

These are called nested squares. What fraction of this shape is shaded in?

![papers/the-kings-school-chester/sample/sample-paper-2/s01-q25-i00.png](/assets/papers/the-kings-school-chester/sample/sample-paper-2/s01-q25-i00.png "papers/the-kings-school-chester/sample/sample-paper-2/s01-q25-i00.png")

</div>
<div class="questioncodeblock" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">
<div class="questioncodeblock_title">
<strong style="display: block; margin-bottom: 0.5rem;">Question Raw</strong>
</div>
<pre class="language-latex"><code class="language-latex">These are called nested squares. What fraction of this shape is shaded in?
<emptyline>
%image%
</code></pre>
</div>
<div class="workings">
<div class="tips placeholder" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Tips**

<div class="tip">
%empty%
</div>
</div>
<div class="formulas placeholder" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Formulas**

<div class="formula">
%empty%
</div>
</div>
<div class="working" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Working**

$
\begin{aligned}
\text {Let the side of the square} \ &= s
\end{aligned}
$

$
\begin{aligned}
\text {Let the area of the square} \ &= a \\\\
                                     &= s^2
\end{aligned}
$

$
\begin{aligned}
\text {Let the side of inner square} \ &= x
\end{aligned}
$

$
\begin{aligned}
\text {Let the area of inner square} \ &= b \\\\
                                       &= x^2
\end{aligned}
$

By pythogoras theorem,

$
\begin{aligned}
(\dfrac{s}{2})^2 + (\dfrac{s}{2})^2 &= x^2 \\\\
\dfrac{s^2}{4} + \dfrac{s^2}{4} &= x^2 \\\\
\dfrac{s^2}{2} &= x^2 \\\\
b &= \dfrac{a}{2}
\end{aligned}
$

Area of inner square $= \dfrac{a}{2}$

Following the similiar pattern,

$
\begin{aligned}
\text {Area of shaded region} \ &= \dfrac{a}{2} - \dfrac{a}{4} + \dfrac{a}{8} - \dfrac{a}{16} + \dfrac{a}{32} \\\\
&= \dfrac{11a}{32}
\end{aligned}
$

$
\begin{aligned}
\text {Fraction of shaded region over total area} \ &= \dfrac{ \dfrac{11a}{32} }{a} \\\\
&= \dfrac{11}{32}
\end{aligned}
$
</div>
</div>
<div class="answers">
<div class="answer" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Answer**

$\dfrac{11}{32}$

</div>
<div class="answer" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Answer**

%empty%

</div>
</div>

<div class="working" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Working**

On examining,

$
\begin{aligned}
\text{Area of Shape A} &= 10 \ \text{unit}^2 \\\\
\text{Shaded Area} &= 2 \ \text{unit}^2 \\\\
\text{Fraction Shaded} &= \dfrac{2}{10} \\\\
&= \dfrac{1 \times \cancel 2}{5 \times \cancel 2} \\\\
&= \dfrac{1}{5} \\\\
                 \\\\
\text{Area of Shape B} &= 16 \ \text{unit}^2 \\\\
\text{Shaded Area} &= 4 \ \text{unit}^2 \\\\
\text{Fraction Shaded} &= \dfrac{4}{16} \\\\
&= \dfrac{1 \times \cancel 4}{4 \times \cancel 4} \\\\
&= \dfrac{1}{4}
\end{aligned}
$

Shape B has a greater fraction shaded than A.
</div>
</div>

<div class="answer" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

Shape B has a greater fraction shaded than A.

$
\begin{aligned}
\text{Total divisions}      &= 12   \\\\
\text{Divisions shaded}     &= 3    \\\\
\text{Percentage shaded}    &= \dfrac {3} {12} \times 100 \\\\
                            &= \dfrac {3 \times 100} {12} \\\\
                            &= \dfrac {3 \times 4 \times 25} {3 \times 4} \\\\
                            &= \dfrac {\cancel{3} \times \cancel{4} \times 25} {\cancel{3} \times \cancel{4}} \\\\
                            &= 25 \%
\end{aligned}
$
</div>

<div class="question subquestion" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Question**

Which is larger, $75\%$ or $\dfrac{5}{8}?$

Give a reason for your answer.

</div>
<div class="questioncodeblock" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">
<div class="questioncodeblock_title">
<strong style="display: block; margin-bottom: 0.5rem;">Question Raw</strong>
</div>
<pre class="language-latex"><code class="language-latex">Which is larger, $75\%$ or $\dfrac{5}{8}?$
<emptyline>
Give a reason for your answer.
</code></pre>
</div>
<div class="workings">
<div class="tips placeholder" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Tips**

<div class="tip">
%empty%
</div>
</div>
<div class="formulas placeholder" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Formulas**

<div class="formula">
%empty%
</div>
</div>
<div class="working" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Working**

$
\begin{aligned}
&= 75\% or \dfrac{5}{8} \\\\
&= \dfrac{75}{100} or \dfrac{5}{8} \\\\
&= \dfrac{25 \times 3}{25 \times 4} or \dfrac{5}{8} \\\\
&= \dfrac{\cancel{25} \times 3}{\cancel{25} \times 4} or \dfrac{5}{8} \\\\
&= \dfrac{3}{4} is greater than \dfrac{5}{8}
\end{aligned}
$
</div>
</div>
<div class="answers">
<div class="answer" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Answer**

$75\%$

</div>
</div>

<div class="tipcodeblock" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

</div>

</code></pre>
</div>

</code></pre>
</div>

<div class="workingcodeblock_title">
<strong style="display: block; margin-bottom: 0.5rem;">Working Raw</strong>
</div>

**Question**

What fraction of the shape shown has been shaded?

![papers/the-perse-school/sample/specimen-paper-4/s01-q08-i00.png](/assets/papers/the-perse-school/sample/specimen-paper-4/s01-q08-i00.png "papers/the-perse-school/sample/specimen-paper-4/s01-q08-i00.png")

<div class="workings">

TODOIMAGE



Show the shaded part in 2 parts so we can add them separately to calculate the shaded part


*ABBR: TODO:: Add drawing ends.*


$
\begin{aligned}
\text{Total boxes}      &= 6 \times 6 \\\\
                        &= 36 \\\\
\text{Shaded boxes}     &= 2 \times 6 + \dfrac{1}{2} \times 3 \times 6 \\\\
                        &= 12 + \dfrac{3 \times 6}{2} \\\\
                        &= 12 + \dfrac{3 \times 3 \times 2}{2} \\\\
                        &= 12 + \dfrac{3 \times 3 \times \cancel{2}}{\cancel{2}} \\\\
                        &= 12 + 9  \\\\
                        &= 21  \\\\
\text{Fraction shaded}  &= \dfrac{21}{36} \\\\
                        &= \dfrac{3 \times 7}{3 \times 12} \\\\
                        &= \dfrac{\cancel{3} \times 7}{\cancel{3} \times 12} \\\\
                        &= \dfrac{7}{12}
\end{aligned}
$
</div>
</div>
<div class="answers">
<div class="answer" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Answer**

$\dfrac {7} {12}$

</div>
</div>

**Question**

What fraction of this shape is shaded?

![papers/the-peterborough-school/sample/sample-paper-1/s01-q04-s01-i00.png](/assets/papers/the-peterborough-school/sample/sample-paper-1/s01-q04-s01-i00.png "papers/the-peterborough-school/sample/sample-paper-1/s01-q04-s01-i00.png")

</div>
<div class="questioncodeblock" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">
<div class="questioncodeblock_title">
<strong style="display: block; margin-bottom: 0.5rem;">Question Raw</strong>
</div>
<pre class="language-latex"><code class="language-latex">What fraction of this shape is shaded?
<emptyline>
%image%
</code></pre>

<div class="workings">
<div class="tips placeholder" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

%empty%
</div>

<div class="working" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Working**

$
\begin{aligned}
\text{Total boxes}    &= 12 \\\\
\text{Shaded boxes}   &= 9 \\\\
\text{Fraction}       &= \dfrac {9} {12} \\\\
                      &= \dfrac {3 \times 3} {3 \times 4} \\\\
                      &= \dfrac {\cancel{3} \times 3} {\cancel{3} \times 4} \\\\
                      &= \dfrac {3} {4}
\end{aligned}
$
</div>
